<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>UMH
PROPERTIES, INC. AND SUBSIDIARIES</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><span id="sk_005"></span>NOTES
TO CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>MARCH
31, 2026 (UNAUDITED)</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>&#160;</b></span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000625" name="us-gaap:OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_805_eus-gaap--OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock_zIqDISmUv395" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">NOTE
1 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82C_zob6NHmkbf2l">ORGANIZATION AND ACCOUNTING POLICIES</span></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">UMH
Properties, Inc., a Maryland corporation, and its subsidiaries (&#8220;we&#8221;, &#8220;our&#8221;, &#8220;us&#8221; or &#8220;the Company&#8221;)
operates as a real estate investment trust (&#8220;REIT&#8221;) deriving its income primarily from real estate rental operations. As of March 31, 2026, the
Company operated a portfolio of <span id="xdx_904_ecustom--NumberOfOperatesManufactureHomeCommunites_iI_uInteger_c20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember_zDtuTTOxNU34" title="Number of operates manufacture home communities"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfOperatesManufactureHomeCommunites" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember" id="Fact000627" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">145</ix:nonFraction></span> manufactured home communities, of which 142 are majority owned and are included in our consolidated
operations with the remaining three owned through our joint ventures with Nuveen Real Estate, in which the Company has a 40% interest.
<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--DescriptionOfBusinessMajorityOwnedCommunities_c20260101__20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember_zNbSHBMX6jGe" title="Description of business majority owned communities"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember" id="Fact000629" name="UMH:DescriptionOfBusinessMajorityOwnedCommunities">Of the 142 majority owned communities, 140 are owned 100% by the Company with the remaining two owned by the Company&#8217;s Opportunity
Zone Fund, in which the Company has a 77% interest.</ix:nonNumeric></span> The Company&#8217;s portfolio of 145 communities contains a total of approximately
<span id="xdx_907_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomeSitesCompanyOwnAndOperates_iI_uInteger_c20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember_zZGcL1UVnDie" title="Number of developed sites"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomeSitesCompanyOwnAndOperates" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember" id="Fact000631" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">27,100</ix:nonFraction></span> developed homesites, of which <span id="xdx_90E_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomeSitesContainRentalHomes_iI_uInteger_c20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember_zFIu1IIXodK4" title="Number of developed sites, contain rental homes"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomeSitesContainRentalHomes" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_RealEstateInvestmentTrustsMember" id="Fact000633" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">11,200</ix:nonFraction></span> contain rental homes that are leased to residents. These 145 communities are located in twelve
states consisting of New Jersey, New York, Ohio, Pennsylvania, Tennessee, Indiana, Maryland, Michigan, Alabama, South Carolina, Florida
and Georgia.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company, through its wholly-owned taxable subsidiary, UMH Sales and Finance, Inc. (&#8220;S&amp;F&#8221;), sells manufactured homes to
residents and prospective residents in our communities. Inherent in the operations of manufactured home communities are site vacancies.
S&amp;F was established to enhance the value of the communities by helping to fill these vacancies through the sales of homes. The Company
holds a <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331__srt--ScheduleOfEquityMethodInvestmentEquityMethodInvesteeNameAxis__custom--OZFundMember_zPmHq4I8479b" title="Investment ownership percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_OZFundMember" id="Fact000635" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">77</ix:nonFraction></span>% controlling interest in its qualified opportunity zone fund which it created in 2022 to acquire, develop and redevelop manufactured
housing communities located in areas designated as Qualified Opportunity Zones by the U.S. Treasury Department to encourage long-term
investment in economically distressed areas. The consolidated financial statements of the Company include S&amp;F and all of its other
wholly-owned subsidiaries and its qualified opportunity zone fund. All intercompany transactions and balances have been eliminated in
consolidation.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
primary focus of our business is the ownership and operation of our manufactured home communities &#8211; leasing of manufactured
homesites and manufactured homes in our communities to residents. The sales of homes are integrated with the leasing of these
manufactured homes and homesites. Management views the Company&#8217;s business as a single segment based on its method of internal
reporting in addition to its allocation of capital and resources. Capital and resources are allocated to further the goal of
maintaining and increasing occupancy and net operating income in our communities. Our chief executive officer, with the assistance
of our chief operating officer, is the principal decision-maker regarding allocation of resources. These decisions are based on the
occupancy of the communities and community net operating income, not based on the performance of home sales. Sales of homes are
necessary to maintain and increase occupancy at our communities. There are no longer dealers selling homes into our communities. We
primarily order homes to fill vacant sites in the communities. These homes are either rented or sold, based on the needs of the
potential residents. Although certain components of the sales operation are tracked (sales,
cost of sales, etc.), separate discrete financial information for the entire sales operation is not available. Most of the personnel costs,
office expenses, maintenance and other expenses are borne by the community and cannot be allocated. The components of the sales operation
play no material role in decisions about resources to be allocated. Resources are allocated to maintaining and increasing occupancy and
net operating income in our communities.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has elected to be taxed as a REIT under Sections 856-860 of the Internal Revenue Code (the &#8220;Code&#8221;) and intends to
maintain its qualification as a REIT in the future. As a qualified REIT, with limited exceptions, the Company will not be taxed under
federal and certain state income tax laws at the corporate level on taxable income that it distributes to its shareholders. For special
tax provisions applicable to REITs, refer to Sections 856-860 of the Code. The Company is subject to franchise taxes in some of the states
in which the Company owns property.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
interim consolidated financial statements furnished herein have been prepared in accordance with accounting principles generally accepted
in the United States of America (&#8220;U.S. GAAP&#8221;) applicable to interim financial information, the instructions to Form 10-Q,
and Article 10 of Regulation S-X. Accordingly, they do not include all of the information and footnotes required by U.S. GAAP for complete
financial statements. In the opinion of management, all adjustments (consisting of normal recurring accruals) considered necessary for
a fair presentation have been included. Operating results for the three months ended March 31, 2026 are not necessarily indicative of
the results that may be expected for the year ending December 31, 2026. For further information, refer to the consolidated financial
statements and footnotes thereto included in the Company&#8217;s annual report on Form 10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000637" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates"><p id="xdx_84E_eus-gaap--UseOfEstimates_zoKpeWzZLKcd" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_862_z1wM3fhbccVd">Use
of Estimates</span></span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
preparing the consolidated financial statements in accordance with U.S. GAAP, management is required to make estimates and assumptions
that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities, as well as contingent assets and liabilities as of the dates of the consolidated
balance sheets and revenue and expenses for the years then ended. These estimates and assumptions include the allowance for doubtful
accounts, valuation of inventory, depreciation, valuation of securities, accounting for land development, reserves and accruals, and
stock compensation expense. Actual results could differ from these estimates and assumptions.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000639" name="us-gaap:PriorPeriodReclassificationAdjustmentDescription"><p id="xdx_849_eus-gaap--PriorPeriodReclassificationAdjustmentDescription_ziByQ07x3s8i" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86B_zMt9gxSXN0Hl">Reclassifications</span>
</span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Certain
amounts in the financial statements for the prior periods have been reclassified to conform to the statement presentation for the current
periods.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i>&#160;</i></span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000641" name="us-gaap:InvestmentPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_848_eus-gaap--InvestmentPolicyTextBlock_zO9elNtmgWs" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_868_z9zI2DUoo7gc">Investment
in Joint Ventures</span></span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.7in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for its investment in entities formed under its joint ventures with Nuveen Real Estate under the equity method of accounting
in accordance with ASC 323, Investments &#8211; Equity Method and Joint Ventures. The Company has the ability to exercise significant
influence, but not control, over the operating and financial decisions of the joint venture entities. Under the equity method of accounting,
the cost of an investment is adjusted for the Company&#8217;s share of the equity in net income or loss from the date of acquisition,
reduced by distributions received and increased by contributions made. The income or loss is allocated in accordance with the provisions
of the operating agreement. The carrying values of the investment in the joint ventures is reviewed for other than temporary impairment
whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate a possible impairment. Financial condition, operational performance, and other economic
trends are among the factors that are considered in evaluation of the existence of impairment indicators (See Note 4).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000643" name="us-gaap:LesseeLeasesPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_841_eus-gaap--LesseeLeasesPolicyTextBlock_zD9vyIThvv05" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86E_zfw1BIYsCIHg">Leases</span></span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for its leases under ASC 842, &#8220;Leases.&#8221; Our primary source of revenue is generated from lease agreements
for our sites and homes, where we are the lessor. These leases are generally for one-year or month-to-month terms and renewable by mutual
agreement from us and the resident, or in some cases, as provided by jurisdictional statute.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company is the lessee in other arrangements, primarily for our corporate office and a ground lease at one community. As of March 31,
2026 and December 31, 2025, the right-of-use assets and corresponding lease liabilities of $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--OperatingLeaseRightOfUseAsset_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--PropertyPlantAndEquipmentByTypeAxis__custom--CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember_zK7pee2In5I5" title="Operating lease right-of-use assets"><span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--OperatingLeaseLiability_iI_pn5n6_c20260331__us-gaap--PropertyPlantAndEquipmentByTypeAxis__custom--CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember_zZxMSJMyeiIc" title="Operating lease liabilities"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingLeaseRightOfUseAsset" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember" id="Fact000645" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingLeaseLiability" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember" id="Fact000647" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">2.6</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> million and $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--OperatingLeaseRightOfUseAsset_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--PropertyPlantAndEquipmentByTypeAxis__custom--CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember_zsNvlTgSZ20h" title="Operating lease right-of-use assets"><span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--OperatingLeaseLiability_iI_pn5n6_c20251231__us-gaap--PropertyPlantAndEquipmentByTypeAxis__custom--CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember_zVHTjlhzCgQ4" title="Operating lease liabilities"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingLeaseRightOfUseAsset" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember" id="Fact000649" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingLeaseLiability" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_CorporateOfficeAndGroundLeaseMember" id="Fact000651" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">2.7</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> million, respectively,
are included in prepaid expenses and other assets and accrued liabilities and deposits on the consolidated balance sheets.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000697" name="us-gaap:RevenueFromContractWithCustomerPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_843_eus-gaap--RevenueFromContractWithCustomerPolicyTextBlock_zoZLdMBkHFbk" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_860_z9cCXEAuI6e">Revenue
Recognition</span></span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for its Sales of Manufactured Homes in accordance with Accounting Standards Update (&#8220;ASU&#8221;) 2014-09 &#8220;Revenue
from Contracts with Customers (Topic 606)&#8221; (ASC 606). For transactions in the scope of ASC 606, we recognize revenue when control
of goods or services transfers to the customer, in the amount that we expect to receive for the transfer of goods or provision of services.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Rental
and related income is generated from lease agreements for our sites and homes. The lease component of these agreements is accounted for
under ASC 842 &#8220;Leases.&#8221; The non-lease components of our lease agreements consist primarily of utility reimbursements, which
are accounted for with the site lease as a single lease under ASC 842.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Revenue
from sales of manufactured homes is recognized in accordance with the core principle of ASC 606, at the time of closing when control
of the home transfers to the customer. After closing of the sale transaction, we generally have no remaining performance obligation.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Interest
income is primarily from notes receivables for the previous sales of manufactured homes. Interest income on these receivables is accrued
based on the unpaid principal balances of the underlying loans on a level yield basis over the life of the loans.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Dividend
income and gain (loss) on sales of marketable securities are from our investments in marketable securities and are presented separately
but are not in the scope of ASC 606.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Other
income primarily consists of brokerage commissions for arranging for the sale of a home by a third party and other miscellaneous income.
This income is recognized when the transactions are completed and our performance obligations have been fulfilled.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000699" name="us-gaap:ReceivablesPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84F_eus-gaap--ReceivablesPolicyTextBlock_zUoi69Tble16" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86A_zJ08MbCp4Gia">Notes
Receivables</span></span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for its receivables in accordance with ASU No. 2016-13, &#8220;Financial Instruments &#8211; Credit Losses (Topic 326):
Measurement of Credit Losses on Financial Instruments.&#8221; ASU 2016-13 requires that entities use a forward looking &#8220;expected
loss&#8221; model that generally will result in the earlier recognition of allowance for credit losses. The measurement of expected credit
losses is based upon historical experience, current conditions, and reasonable and supportable forecasts that affect the collectability
of the reported amount. As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the Company had notes receivable of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--NotesReceivableNet_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zGoehrkXSVql" title="Notes receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesReceivableNet" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000701" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">102.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--NotesReceivableNet_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_zSJdjzsWdPUe" title="Notes receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesReceivableNet" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000703" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">100.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million,
net of the fair value adjustment of $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--NotesReceivableFairValueDisclosure_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_zpe2vWcGnFD7" title="Fair value adjustment of notes receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesReceivableFairValueDisclosure" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000705" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.6</ix:nonFraction></span> million and $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--NotesReceivableFairValueDisclosure_iI_pn5n6_c20251231_z89XPqkJi0fg" title="Fair value adjustment of notes receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesReceivableFairValueDisclosure" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000707" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">1.5</ix:nonFraction></span> million, respectively. Notes receivables are presented as a component of notes
and other receivables, net on our consolidated balance sheets. These receivables represent balances owed to us for previously completed
performance obligations for sales of manufactured homes.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000709" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84C_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zydKnWQzY8ie" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_860_zt2h6nloQIQb">Other
Recent Accounting Pronouncements</span></span></i></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
November 4, 2024, the Financial Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;FASB&#8221;) issued Accounting Standard Update (&#8220;ASU&#8221;)
2024-03 - Income Statement &#8211; Reporting Comprehensive Income - Expense Disaggregation Disclosures (Subtopic 220-40). ASU 2024-03
requires disaggregated disclosure of income statement expenses for public business entities (PBEs). The ASU does not change the expense
captions an entity presents on the face of the income statement; rather, it requires disaggregation of certain expense captions into
specified categories in disclosures within the footnotes to the financial statements. This ASU is effective for annual reporting periods
beginning after December 15, 2026, and interim reporting periods beginning after December 15, 2027 and should be applied either (1) prospectively
to financial statements issued for reporting periods after the effective date of this ASU or (2) retrospectively to any or all prior
periods presented in the financial statements. Early adoption is permitted. The Company anticipates making the required disclosures beginning
with its Form 10-K for the year ending December 31, 2027.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Management
does not believe that any other recently issued, but not yet effective accounting pronouncements, if adopted, would have a material effect
on the accompanying consolidated financial statements.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_854_ziM0We62wUKk" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000711" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerShareTextBlock"><p id="xdx_809_eus-gaap--EarningsPerShareTextBlock_zhMBEztd7V9f" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">NOTE
2 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82F_zueKsXWBKVy5">NET INCOME (LOSS) PER SHARE</span> </span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Basic
Net Income (Loss) per Share is calculated by dividing Net Income (Loss) by the weighted average shares outstanding for the period. Diluted
Net Income (Loss) per Share is calculated by dividing Net Income (Loss) less Income (Loss) Attributable to Non-Controlling Interest by
the weighted average number of common shares outstanding, and when dilutive, the potential net shares that would be issued upon exercise
of stock options pursuant to the treasury stock method. In periods with a net loss, the diluted loss per share equals the basic loss
per share as all common stock equivalents are excluded from the per share calculation because they are anti-dilutive.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For
the three months ended March 31, 2026, common stock equivalents resulting from employee stock options to purchase <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--AntidilutiveSecuritiesExcludedFromComputationOfEarningsPerShareAmount_pn5n6_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--OptionIndexedToIssuersEquityTypeAxis__us-gaap--EmployeeStockOptionMember_zJY7Nd5y9fa9" title="Options to purchase of common stock"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AntidilutiveSecuritiesExcludedFromComputationOfEarningsPerShareAmount" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_EmployeeStockOptionMember" id="Fact000713" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="Shares">6.3</ix:nonFraction></span> million shares
of common stock amounted to <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--WeightedAverageNumberDilutedSharesOutstandingAdjustment_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--OptionIndexedToIssuersEquityTypeAxis__us-gaap--EmployeeStockOptionMember_zwg1zXEQd25l" title="Computation of diluted net income (loss) per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WeightedAverageNumberDilutedSharesOutstandingAdjustment" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_EmployeeStockOptionMember" id="Fact000715" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">373,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares, which were included in the computation of Diluted Net Income per Share. For the three months
ended March 31, 2025, common stock equivalents of <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--WeightedAverageNumberDilutedSharesOutstandingAdjustment_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--OptionIndexedToIssuersEquityTypeAxis__us-gaap--EmployeeStockOptionMember_z4j1VRmXXyM1" title="Computation of diluted net income (loss) per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WeightedAverageNumberDilutedSharesOutstandingAdjustment" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_us-gaap_EmployeeStockOptionMember" id="Fact000717" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">944,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares were excluded from the computation of Diluted Net Loss per Share as
their effect would be anti-dilutive. </span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000719" name="us-gaap:InvestmentsInDebtAndMarketableEquitySecuritiesAndCertainTradingAssetsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_808_eus-gaap--InvestmentsInDebtAndMarketableEquitySecuritiesAndCertainTradingAssetsDisclosureTextBlock_zhgCGlMgcKL5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">NOTE
3 &#8211; <span id="xdx_825_z3VUe7e8HWAd">MARKETABLE SECURITIES</span></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company&#8217;s marketable securities consist primarily of marketable common and preferred stock of other REITs with a fair value of
$<span id="xdx_904_ecustom--AvailableMarketableSecurities_iI_pn5n6_c20260331_z55lPa2LCyy" title="Available marketable securities"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:AvailableMarketableSecurities" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000721" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">26.4</ix:nonFraction></span> million as of March 31, 2026, which represents <span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--PercentageOfUndepreciatedAssets_pid_dp_uPure_c20260101__20260331_zI1E5ygEvwUg" title="Undepreciated assets"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:PercentageOfUndepreciatedAssets" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000723" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">1.2</ix:nonFraction></span>% of undepreciated assets (total assets excluding accumulated depreciation).
Other than purchasing marketable equity securities through automatic dividend reinvestments, the Company has not made any purchases of
REIT securities during the three months ended March 31, 2026 and the Company does not intend to increase its investment in the REIT securities
portfolio. The REIT securities portfolio provides the Company with additional diversification, liquidity and income.</span></p>

4- <span id="xdx_82B_zqwEyJwtlKaj">INVESTMENT IN JOINT VENTURES</span></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
December 2021, the Company and Nuveen Real Estate (&#8220;Nuveen&#8221; or &#8220;Nuveen Real Estate&#8221;), established a joint venture
for the purpose of acquiring manufactured housing and/or recreational vehicle communities that are under development and/or newly developed
and meet certain other investment guidelines. The terms of the initial joint venture entity were set forth in a Limited Liability Company
Agreement dated as of December 8, 2021 (the &#8220;2021 LLC Agreement&#8221;) entered into between a wholly owned subsidiary of the Company
and an affiliate of Nuveen. The 2021 LLC Agreement provided for the parties to initially fund up to $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireBusinessesGross_pn6n6_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_z6rAlbfT1jS" title="Initial commitment gross"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireBusinessesGross" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000735" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">70</ix:nonFraction></span> million of equity capital for
acquisitions during a <span id="xdx_905_ecustom--InitialCommitmentPeriodForAcquisitions_dtM_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zPjm1tg20fKf"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" format="ixt-sec:durmonth" id="Fact000736" name="UMH:InitialCommitmentPeriodForAcquisitions">24</ix:nonNumeric></span>-month commitment period, with Nuveen having the option, subject to certain conditions, to elect to increase
the parties&#8217; total commitments by up to an additional $<span id="xdx_902_ecustom--AdditionalIncreaseInTotalCommitmnets_pn6n6_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zvnZOTd2gSX3" title="Additional increase in total commitments"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:AdditionalIncreaseInTotalCommitmnets" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000738" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-6" scale="6" unitRef="USD">100</ix:nonFraction></span> million and to extend the commitment period for up to an additional
<span id="xdx_902_ecustom--ExtentionForCommitmentPeriod_dc_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zDXdvD1wNJvk"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" format="ixt-sec:durwordsen" id="Fact000739" name="UMH:ExtentionForCommitmentPeriod">four years</ix:nonNumeric></span>. The 2021 LLC Agreement called for committed capital to be funded <span id="xdx_902_ecustom--CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zJ4Yv7tQQknb"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty" contextRef="AsOf2021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000740" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">60</ix:nonFraction></span>% by Nuveen and <span id="xdx_909_ecustom--CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20211208_zRoMLpnYyYMc"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty" contextRef="AsOf2021-12-08" id="Fact000741" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">40</ix:nonFraction></span>% by the Company on a parity basis.
The Company serves as managing member of the joint venture entity and is responsible for day-to-day operations of the joint venture entity
and management of its properties, subject to obtaining approval of Nuveen Real Estate for major decisions (including investments, dispositions,
financings, major capital expenditures and annual budgets). The Company receives property management, asset management and other fees
from the joint venture entity. In addition, once each member has recouped its invested capital and received a <span id="xdx_905_ecustom--UnleveredInternalRateOfReturnPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zkfPMMXXvQxe"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:UnleveredInternalRateOfReturnPercentage" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000742" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">7.5</ix:nonFraction></span>% net unlevered internal

and the Company and Nuveen will receive a promote percentage equal to <span id="xdx_90F_ecustom--ProRataInterestPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20211207__20211208__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zOKCHPEIHPa1"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:ProRataInterestPercentage" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000744" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">70</ix:nonFraction></span>% (in the case of the Company) and <span id="xdx_903_ecustom--ProRataInterestPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zGlyhrmLMiv8"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:ProRataInterestPercentage" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000745" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">30</ix:nonFraction></span>% (in the case of Nuveen)
of the remaining <span id="xdx_908_ecustom--RemainingPromotePercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zbZydRZuwD7c"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:RemainingPromotePercentage" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000746" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">20</ix:nonFraction></span>% of distributable cash. </span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Under
the terms of the 2021 LLC Agreement, after December 8, 2024 or, if later, the second anniversary of the acquisition and placing in service
of a manufactured housing or recreational vehicle community, Nuveen will have a right to initiate the sale of one or more of the communities
owned by the joint venture entity. If Nuveen elects to initiate such a sale process, the Company may exercise a right of first refusal
to acquire Nuveen&#8217;s interest in the community or communities to be sold for a purchase price corresponding to the greater of the
appraised value of such communities or the amount required to provide a <span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--UnleveredInternalRateOfReturnPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20211207__20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zdT1QILH4bOa" title="Unlevered internal rate of return"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:UnleveredInternalRateOfReturnPercentage" contextRef="From2021-12-072021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000750" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">7.5</ix:nonFraction></span>% net unlevered internal rate of return on Nuveen&#8217;s
investment. <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--InvestmentCompanyInternalRateOfReturnAssumptions_c20260101__20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_zmxoYmDT6Bzf" title="Investment company internal rate, description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember" id="Fact000752" name="us-gaap:InvestmentCompanyInternalRateOfReturnAssumptions">In addition, the Company will have the right to buy out Nuveen&#8217;s interest in the joint venture entity at any time after
December 8, 2031 at a purchase price corresponding to the greater of the appraised value of the portfolio or the amount required to provide
a 7.5% net unlevered internal rate of return on Nuveen&#8217;s investment.</ix:nonNumeric></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
2021 LLC Agreement between the Company and Nuveen provided that until the capital contributions to the joint venture are fully funded
or the joint venture is terminated, the joint venture will be the exclusive vehicle for the Company to acquire any manufactured housing
communities and/or recreational vehicle communities that meet the joint venture&#8217;s investment guidelines. These guidelines called
for the joint venture to acquire manufactured housing and recreational vehicle communities that have been developed within the previous
<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--JointVentureToAcquiredManufacturedHousingAndRecreationalVehicleCommunities_dc_c20260101__20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--LLCAgreementMember_z3A8wM26yoYj" title="Joint venture to acquired manufactured housing and recreational vehicle communities"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_LLCAgreementMember" format="ixt-sec:durwordsen" id="Fact000754" name="UMH:JointVentureToAcquiredManufacturedHousingAndRecreationalVehicleCommunities">two years</ix:nonNumeric></span> and are less than 20% occupied, are located in certain geographic markets, are projected to meet certain cash flow and internal
rate of return targets, and satisfy certain other criteria. The Company agreed to offer Nuveen the opportunity to have the joint venture
acquire any manufactured housing community or recreational vehicle community that meets these investment guidelines. Under the terms
of the 2021 LLC Agreement, if Nuveen determines not to pursue or approve any such acquisition, the Company would be permitted to acquire
the property outside the joint venture. Since the execution of the 2021 LLC Agreement, Nuveen has provided the Company with written waivers
of the exclusivity provision of the 2021 LLC Agreement with regard to two property acquisitions that may have fit the investment guidelines
of the joint venture, which permitted the Company to acquire them outside of the Nuveen joint venture. Except for investment opportunities
that are offered to and declined by Nuveen, the Company is prohibited from developing, owning, operating or managing manufactured housing
communities or recreational vehicle communities within a 10-mile radius of any community owned by the joint venture. However, this restriction
does not apply with respect to investments by the Company in existing communities operated by the Company.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
2021 LLC Agreement provides that Nuveen will have the right to remove and replace the Company as managing member of the joint venture
and manager of the joint venture&#8217;s properties if the Company breaches certain obligations or certain events occur. Upon such removal,
Nuveen may elect to buy out the Company&#8217;s interest in the joint venture at <span id="xdx_90C_ecustom--PercentageOfInterestElectToBuyOutInJointVenture_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_zjbviGnjBZT1" title="Percentage of share elect to buy out in joint venture"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:PercentageOfInterestElectToBuyOutInJointVenture" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember" id="Fact000756" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">98</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the value of the Company&#8217;s interest in
the joint venture. If Nuveen does not exercise such buy-out right, the Company may, at specified times, elect to initiate a sale of the
communities owned by the joint venture, subject to a right of first refusal on the part of Nuveen. The 2021 LLC Agreement contains restrictions
on a party&#8217;s right to transfer its interest in the joint venture without the approval of the other party.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
2021 LLC Agreement requires the Company to offer Nuveen the opportunity to have the joint venture acquire a manufactured housing community
or recreational vehicle community that meets the investment guidelines. If Nuveen decides not to acquire the community through the joint
venture, however, the Company is free to purchase the community on its own outside of the joint venture. </span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
December 2021, the joint venture entity formed under the 2021 LLC Agreement closed on the acquisition of Sebring Square, a newly developed
all-age, manufactured home community located in Sebring, Florida, for a total purchase price of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireProductiveAssets_pn5n6_c20211201__20211231__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_zRDqgdylYqhk"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireProductiveAssets" contextRef="From2021-12-012021-12-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember" id="Fact000757" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">22.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million. This community contains
<span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomesites_uInteger_c20211201__20211231__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--SebringSquareMember_zm3wgPhPosG5"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomesites" contextRef="From2021-12-012021-12-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_SebringSquareMember" id="Fact000758" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">219</ix:nonFraction></span> developed homesites situated on approximately <span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--AreaOfLand_iI_uAcre_c20211231__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--SebringSquareMember_z4MaF3xWdIil" title="Area of land"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AreaOfLand" contextRef="AsOf2021-12-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_SebringSquareMember" id="Fact000760" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Acre">39</ix:nonFraction></span> acres. In December 2022, this joint venture entity closed on the acquisition of
Rum Runner, another newly developed all-age, manufactured home community also located in Sebring, Florida for a total purchase price
of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireProductiveAssets_pn5n6_c20221201__20221231__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_zklRT75hrJ1g" title="Payments to acquire productive assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireProductiveAssets" contextRef="From2022-12-012022-12-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember" id="Fact000762" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">15.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million. This community contains <span id="xdx_908_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomesites_uInteger_c20221201__20221231__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--RumRunnerMember_zHgFqCYuifW"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomesites" contextRef="From2022-12-012022-12-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_RumRunnerMember" id="Fact000763" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">144</ix:nonFraction></span> developed homesites situated on approximately <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--AreaOfLand_iI_uAcre_c20221231__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--RumRunnerMember_zz6VjQfbXfmb" title="Area of land"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AreaOfLand" contextRef="AsOf2022-12-31_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_RumRunnerMember" id="Fact000765" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Acre">20</ix:nonFraction></span> acres. The Company manages these communities
on behalf of the joint venture entity.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"></p>

    <div style="margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->15<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">During
the time since the joint venture with Nuveen was first established in 2021, the Company and Nuveen have continued to seek opportunities
to acquire additional manufactured housing and/or recreational vehicle communities that are under development and/or newly developed
and meet certain other investment guidelines. During 2022, the Company and Nuveen informally agreed that any future acquisitions would
be made by one or more new joint venture entities to be formed for that purpose and that the original joint venture entity formed in
December 2021 will not consummate additional acquisitions but will maintain its existing property portfolio, consisting of the Sebring
Square and Rum Runner communities. The Company and Nuveen also informally agreed that, unless otherwise determined in connection with
any specific future investment, capital for any such new joint venture entity would continue to be funded <span id="xdx_909_ecustom--CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20211208__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember_zJDEG5Tzoemk" title="Committed capital percent by related party"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty" contextRef="AsOf2021-12-08_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_TwoThousandTwentyOneLLCAgreementMember" id="Fact000767" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">60</ix:nonFraction></span>% by Nuveen and <span id="xdx_906_ecustom--CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20211208_zqn9PGVtMk1l" title="Committed capital percent by related party"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:CommittedCapitalPercentByRelatedParty" contextRef="AsOf2021-12-08" id="Fact000769" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">40</ix:nonFraction></span>% by the
Company on a parity basis and that other terms would be similar to those of the 2021 LLC Agreement, except that the amounts of the parties&#8217;
respective capital commitments will be determined on a property-by-property basis.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
November 2023, the Company expanded its relationship with Nuveen Real Estate and formed a second joint venture entity with Nuveen. The
new joint venture entity was established to, directly or through one or more subsidiaries, identify, source, originate, acquire, hold,
operate, sell, lease, mortgage, maintain, own, manage, finance, refinance, reposition, improve, renovate, develop, redevelop, pledge,
hedge, exchange, and otherwise deal in and with the rental of manufactured housing and/or recreational vehicle communities that meet
other investment guidelines. The terms of the new joint venture entity are set forth in a Limited Liability Company Agreement dated as
of November 29, 2023 (the &#8220;2023 LLC Agreement&#8221;) entered into between a wholly owned subsidiary of the Company and an affiliate
of Nuveen. The Company serves as managing member of this new joint venture entity and is responsible for day-to-day operations of the
joint venture entity and management of its properties, subject to obtaining approval of Nuveen Real Estate for major decisions (including
investments, dispositions, financings, major capital expenditures and annual budgets). The Company receives property management oversight,
development and other fees from the joint venture entity. Sixty-one acres of land located in Honey Brook, Pennsylvania, previously owned
by the Company, with a carrying value cost basis of $<span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--InvestmentsOfCostBasis_iI_pn5n6_c20231130_zGMGznOWxrA7"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:InvestmentsOfCostBasis" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-30" id="Fact000770" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">3.8</ix:nonFraction></span> million, was contributed to the new joint venture entity. The Company was reimbursed
by Nuveen for <span id="xdx_906_ecustom--ReimburseOfCarryingValueOfLand_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20231130_zWy76ZbA0M84" title="Reimburse of carrying value of land"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:ReimburseOfCarryingValueOfLand" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-30" id="Fact000772" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">60</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the carrying value of this land. This new joint venture entity is focused on the development and operation of a
new manufactured housing community on this property. The community contains <span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomesites_uInteger_c20231130__20231130__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--NuveenRealEstateMember_zHcXI2Gzi0B2"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomesites" contextRef="From2023-11-302023-11-30_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_NuveenRealEstateMember" id="Fact000773" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">113</ix:nonFraction></span> manufactured home sites situated on approximately <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--AreaOfLand_iI_uAcre_c20231130__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--NuveenRealEstateMember_zrUzbu8i2kF5"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AreaOfLand" contextRef="AsOf2023-11-30_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_NuveenRealEstateMember" id="Fact000774" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Acre">61</ix:nonFraction></span>
acres. This community, named Honey Ridge, opened for occupancy in June 2025 and currently has <span id="xdx_90E_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomesites_uInteger_c20250601__20250630__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--NuveenRealEstateMember_zKJUOx7fbCWh"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomesites" contextRef="From2025-06-012025-06-30_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_NuveenRealEstateMember" id="Fact000775" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">32</ix:nonFraction></span> homes on-site of which <span id="xdx_908_ecustom--NumberOfDevelopedHomeSold_uInteger_c20250601__20250630__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--NuveenRealEstateMember_zC7appf5tK3j" title="Number of homes sold"><ix:nonFraction name="UMH:NumberOfDevelopedHomeSold" contextRef="From2025-06-012025-06-30_custom_NuveenGlobalInvestmentsLLCMember_custom_NuveenRealEstateMember" id="Fact000777" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Integer">15</ix:nonFraction></span> have been
sold. </span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">References
in this report to the Company&#8217;s joint venture relationships with Nuveen are intended to refer to its ongoing relationships with
Nuveen. </span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for its joint ventures with Nuveen Real Estate under the equity method of accounting in accordance with ASC 323, &#8220;Investments
&#8211; Equity Method and Joint Ventures&#8221;. </span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000779" name="UMH:OpportunityZoneFundTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80A_ecustom--OpportunityZoneFundTextBlock_zCWcx9Fg6ER1" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">NOTE
5 - <span id="xdx_823_zO119oaStaMa">OPPORTUNITY ZONE FUND</span></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
July 2022, the Company invested $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireInvestments_pn5n6_c20220701__20220731__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--UMHOZFundLLCMember_zsWC0CV762Vc" title="Payment for investments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireInvestments" contextRef="From2022-07-012022-07-31_custom_UMHOZFundLLCMember" id="Fact000781" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">8.0</ix:nonFraction></span> million, representing a portion of the capital gain the Company recognized as a result of the Monmouth
Real Estate Investment Corp. (&#8220;MREIC&#8221;) merger, in the UMH OZ Fund, LLC (&#8220;OZ Fund&#8221;), a new entity formed by the
Company. The OZ Fund was created to acquire, develop and redevelop manufactured housing communities requiring substantial capital investment
and located in areas designated as Qualified Opportunity Zones by the Treasury Department pursuant to a program authorized under the
2017 Tax Cuts and Jobs Act to encourage long-term investment in economically distressed areas. The OZ Fund was designed to allow the
Company and other investors in the OZ Fund to defer the tax on recently realized capital gains reinvested in the OZ Fund until December
31, 2026 and to potentially obtain certain other tax benefits. <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--ManagementFeeDescription_c20220701__20220731_zPIR9nXvR77f" title="Management fee description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2022-07-012022-07-31" id="Fact000783" name="us-gaap:ManagementFeeDescription">UMH manages the OZ Fund and will receive certain management fees as well
as a 15% carried interest in distributions by the OZ Fund to the other investors when earned and realized (subject to first returning
investor capital with a 5% preferred return). UMH will have a right of first offer to purchase the communities from the OZ Fund at the
time of sale at their then-current appraised value.</ix:nonNumeric></span> On August 10, 2022, the Company, through the OZ Fund, acquired Garden View Estates,
located in Orangeburg, South Carolina, for approximately $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireOtherRealEstate_pn5n6_c20220809__20220810__srt--RealEstateAndAccumulatedDepreciationDescriptionOfPropertyAxis__custom--GardenViewEstatesMember__srt--StatementGeographicalAxis__custom--OrangeburgSouthCarolinaMember_zV5J8Lu9rHdh" title="Payments to acquire real estate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireOtherRealEstate" contextRef="From2022-08-092022-08-10_custom_GardenViewEstatesMember_custom_OrangeburgSouthCarolinaMember" id="Fact000785" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">5.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million. On January 19, 2023, the Company, through the OZ Fund, acquired
Mighty Oak, located in Albany, Georgia, for approximately $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireOtherRealEstate_pn5n6_c20230118__20230119__srt--RealEstateAndAccumulatedDepreciationDescriptionOfPropertyAxis__custom--MightyOakMember__srt--StatementGeographicalAxis__custom--AlbanyGeorgiaMember_zFlujTFnelzc" title="Payments to acquire real estate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireOtherRealEstate" contextRef="From2023-01-182023-01-19_custom_MightyOakMember_custom_AlbanyGeorgiaMember" id="Fact000787" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">3.7</ix:nonFraction></span> million. As of March 31, 2026, the Company&#8217;s investment in the OZ
Fund represented <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage_iI_pid_dp_uPure_c20260331__srt--ScheduleOfEquityMethodInvestmentEquityMethodInvesteeNameAxis__custom--OZFundMember_z2nh5YHG64t1" title="Investment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_OZFundMember" id="Fact000789" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">77</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the total capital contributed to the OZ Fund and is consolidated in the Company&#8217;s Consolidated Financial
Statements. Other investors in the OZ Fund include certain officers, directors and employees of the Company, who invested on the same
terms as other investors. </span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; margin-left: 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.5in; background-color: white"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
